\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{fxe6}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{fxe6} (\texttt{f7e6}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#41} (ply 17) [structural distance $d$=54, QAP obj=95]: the master played \texttt{bxc6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pf2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pb2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}g2 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{P}e7 as their \emph{B}c8; transferred move \texttt{f7e6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#42} (ply 17) [structural distance $d$=54, QAP obj=95]: the master played \texttt{bxc6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pf2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pb2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}g2 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{P}e7 as their \emph{B}c8; transferred move \texttt{f7e6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0001\#256} (ply 13) [structural distance $d$=50, QAP obj=95]: the master played \texttt{bxc6}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pf2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pb2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{P}g6 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{B}g7 as their \emph{P}e7; transferred move \texttt{f7e6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 15 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{fxe6} (summed QAP objective 1374, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
